    <p class="mb-1" >
        <a class="Link--primary text-bold js-navigation-open markdown-title" href="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/commit/ff487dd31bc56cb75925be2dba8dda1988554672">Document that service classes from dependencies must be indexed</a>

        <span class="hidden-text-expander inline">
            <button aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Commit message body" type="button" data-view-component="true" class="ellipsis-expander js-details-target btn">    &hellip;

      <div class="my-2 Details-content--hidden" ><pre class="text-small ws-pre-wrap">A new page under "Publish and configure SOAP services" explains that the
whole class hierarchy of a service implementation must be in the Jandex
index, shows the build failure when a service interface from a
dependency is missing, and explains how to index that dependency.

The error message linked to {link-quarkus-docs-base}, an AsciiDoc
attribute that Java does not resolve, so it now has the full URL.
